"""Debug script with detailed tracing."""

from sqlfluff.core.templaters.jinja import JinjaTemplater
from sqlfluff.core.config import FluffConfig
import regex

# Test SQL content - simpler version
test_sql = """{% set max_partitions = 2 %}
{% for i in range(max_partitions) %}{% set useless1 = 0 %}{% set useless2 = 0 %}{% set useless3 = 0 %}{% set useless4 = 0 %}{% set useless5 = 0 %}
    SELECT * FROM my_table
    WHERE partition_key = {{ i }};
{% endfor %}"""

def main():
    print("=== Detailed Debugging ===")
    print("Input SQL:")
    print(repr(test_sql))
    print()
    
    # Create templater and config
    templater = JinjaTemplater()
    config = FluffConfig(overrides={"dialect": "ansi"})
    
    # Manually construct analyzer and tracer to examine internals
    from sqlfluff.core.templaters.slicers.tracer import JinjaAnalyzer
    
    env, live_context, render_func = templater.construct_render_func(
        fname="test.sql", config=config
    )
    
    analyzer = JinjaAnalyzer(test_sql, env)
    tracer = analyzer.analyze(render_func)
    
    # Show trace template
    trace_template_str = "".join(
        (
            tracer.raw_slice_info[rs].alternate_code
            if tracer.raw_slice_info[rs].alternate_code is not None
            else rs.raw
        )
        for rs in tracer.raw_sliced
    )
    
    print("Trace template string:")
    print(repr(trace_template_str))
    print()
    
    # Show trace template output
    trace_template_output = render_func(trace_template_str)
    print("Trace template output:")
    print(repr(trace_template_output))
    print()
    
    # Parse trace entries manually to understand the pattern
    trace_entries = list(regex.finditer(r"\0", trace_template_output))
    print(f"Number of trace entries: {len(trace_entries)}")
    
    entries_data = []
    for match_idx, match in enumerate(trace_entries):
        pos1 = match.span()[0]
        try:
            pos2 = trace_entries[match_idx + 1].span()[0]
        except IndexError:
            pos2 = len(trace_template_output)
        p = trace_template_output[pos1 + 1 : pos2]
        m_id = regex.match(r"^([0-9a-f]+)(_(\d+))?", p)
        if m_id:
            if m_id.group(3):
                alt_id, slice_length = m_id.group(1), int(m_id.group(3))
                print(f"  Entry {match_idx}: ID={alt_id}, length={slice_length}")
                entries_data.append((alt_id, slice_length))
            else:
                alt_id, slice_length = m_id.group(0), len(p[len(m_id.group(0)) + 1 :])
                print(f"  Entry {match_idx}: ID={alt_id}, length={slice_length} (code)")
                entries_data.append((alt_id, slice_length))
        else:
            print(f"  Entry {match_idx}: Could not parse {p}")
    
    print()
    
    # Count occurrences of each ID
    id_counts = {}
    id_lengths = {}
    for alt_id, length in entries_data:
        if alt_id not in id_counts:
            id_counts[alt_id] = 0
            id_lengths[alt_id] = []
        id_counts[alt_id] += 1
        id_lengths[alt_id].append(length)
    
    print("ID occurrence counts:")
    for alt_id, count in id_counts.items():
        total_length = sum(id_lengths[alt_id])
        print(f"  ID {alt_id}: {count} times, lengths={id_lengths[alt_id]}, total={total_length}")
    
    print()
    
    # Show mapping of IDs to slices
    print("ID to slice mapping:")
    id_to_slice_idx = {}
    for i, raw_slice in enumerate(tracer.raw_sliced):
        slice_info = tracer.raw_slice_info[raw_slice]
        if slice_info.unique_alternate_id:
            id_to_slice_idx[slice_info.unique_alternate_id] = i
            print(f"  Slice {i} (ID {slice_info.unique_alternate_id}): {raw_slice}")

if __name__ == "__main__":

        slice_contributions: Dict[int, List[int]] = {}
        
        # Collect all trace entries first
        trace_entries_data = []
        for match_idx, match in enumerate(trace_entries):
            pos1 = match.span()[0]
            try:
                pos2 = trace_entries[match_idx + 1].span()[0]
            except IndexError:
                pos2 = len(trace_template_output)
            p = trace_template_output[pos1 + 1 : pos2]
            m_id = regex.match(r"^([0-9a-f]+)(_(\d+))?", p)
            if not m_id:
                raise ValueError(  # pragma: no cover
                    "Internal error. Trace template output does not match expected "
                    "format."
                )
            if m_id.group(3):
                # E.g. "00000000000000000000000000000001_83". The number after
                # "_" is the length (in characters) of a corresponding literal
                # in raw_str.
                alt_id, slice_length = m_id.group(1), int(m_id.group(3))
            else:
                # E.g. "00000000000000000000000000000002 a < 10". The characters
                # after the slice ID are executable code from raw_str.
                alt_id, slice_length = m_id.group(0), len(p[len(m_id.group(0)) + 1 :])

            target_slice_idx = self.find_slice_index(alt_id)
            trace_entries_data.append((target_slice_idx, slice_length, alt_id))
            
            # Track contributions for non-zero lengths
            if slice_length > 0:
                if target_slice_idx not in slice_contributions:
                    slice_contributions[target_slice_idx] = []
                slice_contributions[target_slice_idx].append(slice_length)
        
        # Process trace entries with special handling for slices that contribute multiple times
        for entry_idx, (target_slice_idx, slice_length, alt_id) in enumerate(trace_entries_data):
            target_inside_block = self.raw_slice_info[
                self.raw_sliced[target_slice_idx]
            ].inside_block
            
            # Check if this slice contributes content and appears multiple times
            if (slice_length > 0 and 
                target_slice_idx in slice_contributions and 
                len(slice_contributions[target_slice_idx]) > 1):
                
                # This slice contributes content multiple times. We need to be careful
                # about how we handle this to maintain compatibility with existing behavior
                # while fixing the length validation issue.
                
                # Count how many times we've seen this slice so far
                occurrences_so_far = 0
                for prev_entry_idx in range(entry_idx):
                    if trace_entries_data[prev_entry_idx][0] == target_slice_idx:
                        occurrences_so_far += 1
                
                total_contributions = len(slice_contributions[target_slice_idx])
                
                if occurrences_so_far == total_contributions - 1:
                    # This is the LAST occurrence of this slice.
                    # Instead of recording just this slice's length, we record 
                    # the accumulated length of ALL contributions of this slice.
                    accumulated_length = sum(slice_contributions[target_slice_idx])
                    adjusted_length = accumulated_length
                else:
                    # This is NOT the last occurrence. 
                    # To avoid double-counting in the final length validation,
                    # we record zero length for this occurrence.
                    adjusted_length = 0
            else:
                # Normal case: single occurrence or zero length
                adjusted_length = slice_length

            # DEBUG: Print what we're doing
            # print(f"Processing entry {entry_idx}: slice_idx={target_slice_idx}, orig_len={slice_length}, adj_len={adjusted_length}")

            if not target_inside_block:
                # Normal case: Walk through the template.
                self.move_to_slice(target_slice_idx, adjusted_length)
            else:
                # {% block %} executes code elsewhere in the template but does
                # not move there. It's a bit like macro invocation.
                self.record_trace(adjusted_length, target_slice_idx)</arg_value><arg_key>old_str</arg_key><arg_value>        # Keep track of slice contributions to handle loop cases properly
